Later corrections supersede the retained first proposal.\n\n## Resolve the kind of protection first\n\nGuardian Pulse changes the target of an attack before comparing its original roll with AC. The Guardian becomes the actual hit/miss and on-hit-rider target. One attack can be retargeted once. Multiple offers are chosen by the original intended target; only the selected Guardian spends its Reaction/use. After the incoming attack fully resolves, a surviving, eligible Guardian may make the printed counterattack against an attacker in reach whether the original attack hit or missed. This free attack cannot grant another free attack or Action. Retargeting does not spend the later damage-transfer opportunity.\n\nBloodbond and Veinlock Tether divide the finalized raw damage before either recipient's mitigation. Attack/save success, save-halving and critical dice have already determined that amount. Bloodbond transfers one quarter, rounded down; Veinlock instead chooses up to S+M. Each recipient applies its own eligible defenses once to its own share.\n\nOther supplied damage transfers divide damage after the original target's defenses and legal pure prevention, before temporary HP. Their transferred portions keep damage type and skip another DR/resistance/immunity/prevention stage. Temporary HP, applicable HP floors, would-die replacements, Wounds, concentration and ordinary life consequences still apply. “Unmitigated damage” does not mean “death cannot be prevented.”\n\nPure prevention removes damage without creating a second recipient. Elemental Absorption adds its resistance during ordinary defenses. Aura of Bonds and Crown reductions use the strongest applicable class flat reduction, then resistance/immunity. Finite/reactive prevention such as Bloom and Ancestral Guardian follows those defenses before post-defense transfer and temporary HP. Verify additional item/spell sources before treating this as universal defense ordering.\n\nRedirecting Palm prevents half the Monk's post-defense damage, rounded down. The Monk keeps the remainder. A second enemy distinct from the triggering melee attacker, within5feet, can receive new force damage equal to the prevented amount, capped at Monk level. That new damage gets its own defenses; it is not a transferred attack or critical hit. With no second enemy, the Monk still receives the reduction.\n\nDirect HP payments from Blood Sacrifice, Flesh-to-RP, Profane Exchange and Channel Life stay outside the damage sequence. They bypass temporary HP, obey their own legal minimum HP and do not create damage/Wound/resource triggers from paying the cost. Healing-recipient substitution and excess-healing conversion also remain distinct operations.\n\n## One transfer operation and exact amounts\n\nA root here means one original recipient's damage packet. A shared area cast has a separate root for each original recipient, under the same cast/roll identity.\n\nOne such root accepts one damage-sharing operation across all owners. A pre-mitigation split locks both its retained original portion and transferred portions against later transfer. Burden Shared can divide one operation among up to two other explicitly willing recipients. It does not create two redirection events. No transferred recipient may be the original damage target.\n\n**New stage-precedence integration:** consider pre-mitigation offers before applying defenses, and post-defense offers afterward. Within the same stage, select eligible positive mandatory/automatic protection before optional offers; competing mandatory claims are chosen by the original damage target. A legally chosen Bloodbond/Veinlock operation can therefore preempt later Aegis/Final Stand because that root is already claimed. Do not preview or consume defenses/Bloom to decide a later-stage preference. The helper accepts an already selected legal operation; it does not select Reactions or schedule automatic offers.\n\nOptional transfer requires a positive chosen amount and available damage. Zero transfer cannot spend an optional Reaction/resource/use. A mandatory rounded-zero share does nothing and spends no transfer allowance. An independently legal prevention component can still occur.\n\n| Source | Transferred amount |\n|---|---|\n| Bloodbond | floor(raw damage/4), fixed |\n| Veinlock Tether / Shared Breath / Selfless Guardian | chosen up to S+M |\n| Protective Stance / Emergency Protocols | min(S+M, available damage), fixed |\n| Life Linked | chosen up to floor(available damage/2) |\n| Guardian Tissue | floor(available damage/3), fixed |\n| Aegis Incarnate / Martyr's Share | floor(available damage/2), fixed |\n| Chainbound Smite | chosen up to max(3, actual CHA modifier) |\n| Linked Ward | chosen up to max(2, twice actual CHA modifier) |\n| King's Smite protection | chosen up to5 |\n| Aura of the Crown | chosen up to M |\n| Crown of Embers / Burden Shared | chosen up to level+M |\n| Crown Undying | chosen up to remaining level+M round allowance |\n| Final Stand | all available damage, subject to shared allowance |\n\nEvery amount is capped by the available packet at its source's stage. Fixed fractions/fixed protection cannot voluntarily choose a smaller amount. Original actions, range, willingness, costs, active-state requirements, cadence and source-specific use limits still apply.\n\n## Hit identity and final damage accounting\n\nThe original damage target retains its original hit, failed-save, critical and on-hit-rider identity even if all damage is transferred. A rider that requires positive damage must recheck the actual remaining packet. A transferred recipient was not hit by that attack and did not fail its save; it receives no attached poison/control/maiming/knockout rider merely because damage moved.\n\nAccordingly, transferred damage from a critical attack gives an already0HP recipient the ordinary one damage failure, not two critical-hit failures. Concentration still sees the transferred recipient's resolved packet even if temporary HP absorbs all of it.\n\nUse the [reviewed life-state interface](HEALING_DEATH_PLAYTEST.md) after resolving each final recipient. Numeric actual damage is temporary HP absorbed plus actual normal HP lost, excluding overkill and damage prevented by an HP floor. Sum each terminal recipient once. Do not also count the unsplit parent or unrelated counterdamage.\n\nPhysical root totals include every recipient. A healing/resource/reflection consumer sums only branches permitted for that consumer and qualifying for its own source's target predicate. For example, a meaningful-hostile-only drain does not automatically count damage transferred onto a friendly protector. Check all applicable flags for a compound trigger, such as a passive healing proc.\n\n## Source-specific restrictions\n\nAll transfers prohibit another transfer. All supplied post-defense transfers prohibit another mitigation/prevention stage. Further restrictions differ:\n\n| Source family | Additional restrictions on transferred damage |\n|---|---|\n| Paladin engine | No reflection or resource generation; does not universally prohibit other retaliation or healing |\n| Protective Stance | Also no generic damage-trigger chains |\n| Burden Shared | Also no derived healing |\n| Oracle Life Linked | No reflection, resource/RC, passive, condition, action or copy generation; no recovery or retaliation |\n| Guardian Tissue / Aegis | No BT generation; no invented ban on every other resource |\n| Shared Breath | No additional generic trigger prohibition |\n| Selfless Guardian / Emergency Protocols | No retaliation chain |\n| Bloodbond / Veinlock | Each recipient still has its one defense stage; no additional blanket prohibition |\n\nA no-derived-healing flag does not prevent unrelated later healing of the recipient.\n\nThe source's explicit redirection riders remain narrowly available. Unbroken Covenant triggers on the redirection operation, even when Protective Stance prohibits generic damage triggers; use half the final protector actual damage, capped at S+M. These self-absorption riders require damage actually transferred to the redirecting owner, not to another ally under Burden Shared. Linked Ward's own transfer granting temporary HP on reducing the Paladin to0 uses final actual damage, capped at level+M. Final Stand also gives the protected ally temporary HP equal to Paladin level and resistance to all damage through that ally's next turn end; the numeric helper handles only temporary HP, and the caller must create the resistance record. None grants a generic permission to ignore other source restrictions. Temporary HP still uses the larger remaining amount and does not stack.\n\n## Paladin round ledger\n\nTrack each Paladin owner's numbered-round events. Ordinarily, one redirection event is available, using exactly one Paladin redirection feature. Final Stand is automatic on the first eligible positive post-defense packet while its allowance remains; the Paladin cannot save it for a later hit. Its “all damage” text does not override the explicit engine ceiling. It may redirect again in a later numbered round if still active before its source-next-start deadline.\n\nCrown Undying can raise the owner's total to two different damage events, with at most one ordinary/non-Crown feature. The additional permission belongs to Crown Undying. A prior ordinary redirection remains recorded if Crown activates later in the same round; only one further Crown event is then possible. Reapplication does not clear history.\n\nThe two Crown events may protect the same selected ally. Sum Crown-transferred amounts against level+M per round. Its heal is floor(final Paladin branch actual damage/2) for each event, then capped by the remaining S+M per protected ally that round. Commit that nominal source entitlement before target healing restrictions; blocked or capped healing refunds no event, transfer amount or healing entitlement. The heal cannot heal the Paladin or start another redirection/heal-copy chain.\n\n## Bloom and devices\n\nBloom Ward is a finite10+Oracle-level prevention pool across packets. Apply its legal prevention after ordinary defenses. Subtract only the prevented amount. The bloom ends on depletion, its1minute expiry, or actual legal removal. Suppression alone does not end it. Ending grants the printed +1 Frightened-save rider through the target's next end. A late clock observation must retain the endpoint calculated from the actual expiry, not create a fresh duration. An unmitigated transferred branch cannot consume this prevention pool.\n\nEmergency Protocols requires an active/conscious drone within its printed range. The transfer occurs after the Tinker's defenses and before temporary HP. The drone receives the bounded transferred amount without mitigation; legally granted temporary HP can absorb it. At0HP the drone is destroyed and receives no organic Wound or death-save procedure.\n\nTinker engine[2] retains the destroyed drone at0 until its explicit repair/rebuild procedure. Ordinary functional repair cannot reconstruct a destroyed drone or reset ammunition, uses, duration or command budget. This package's device helper only resolves damage/destruction; it does not implement reconstruction or grant a destruction reward. The broader companion/device lifecycle remains open.\n\n## Reference boundary\n\nThe helper validates arithmetic, stages, branch identity, replay protection and round entitlements. Callers establish legal sources, target relationships, exact source cadence, action/resource payment, clock endpoints and source-native riders. No helper imports card ownership merely from a string identifier. The source register preserves every exact original procedure alongside the overlay.\n\nGuardian Pulse's new counterattack carries its explicit no-further-free-attack/no-Action limits, without inheriting unrelated incoming healing/resource/reflection restrictions. Palm's force damage has no invented copy/action/resource prohibition; each downstream source determines eligibility. It cannot trigger another Palm because it is not a melee attack.\n\nValidation currently covers94 targeted checks and186 conservation states. Those6 spells are now operational playtest drafts; the catalog has78 operational and770 unfinished entries. This is a bounded interface, not DD24 completion:131 additional semantic matches and healing content in other domains still need dispositions. Original cards/books are preserved.\n\n[Exact source procedures](HEALING_DEATH_SOURCE_BINDINGS.md) · [Latest boundary decisions](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_HEALING_FINAL_BOUNDARIES.md) · [Supplement decisions](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_HEALING_SUPPLEMENT_REVIEW.md) · [Reference checks](HEALING_PLAYTEST_VALIDATION.json) · [Source integrity](HEALING_DEATH_SOURCE_VALIDATION.json)\n\n## Distinct life operations\n\n| Operation | Result | Restriction interface |\n|---|---|---|\n| Minimum-HP prevention | Preserve positiveHP instead of crossing a floor | Not HP gain; cannot create positiveHP from0 |\n| Ordinary healing | Increase a living creature's normalHP | Apply modifiers, prohibitions, reserve and Critical ceiling |\n| Direct state recovery | Specifically defined dying/Stable/HP recovery | Broad cannot-regain-HP applies; ordinary healing modifiers do not |\n| Would-die replacement | Replace a pending death before it becomes final | Exact source eligibility; not a generic damage floor |\n| Resurrection | Actually dead willing identity returns at printedHP | Separate life transition; actual corpse/soul blockers apply |\n| Companion reconstruction | Restore a device/companion through its own source | No general creature resurrection permission |\n| TemporaryHP | Separate nonstacking buffer | Not healing, stabilization or resurrection |\n\nNames never select the operation. Phoenix Rebirth heals a still-living ally at0. Cleric Resurrection returns an actual dead identity. Ritual of Undeath's Ward replaces death; ordinary Death Ward is a positive-to-zero floor. Feign death and spirit manifestations are not death or resurrection.\n\n## Damage quantities and resolution\n\n| Quantity | Definition and use |\n|---|---|\n| resolvedDamage | After defenses, before tempHP; positive damage event iff greater than0 |\n| tempAbsorbed | min(tempHP, resolvedDamage) |\n| postTempDamage | resolvedDamage minus tempAbsorbed, uncapped by currentHP; Core Instant Death/Wounds |\n| actualHpDamage | NormalHP lost to the packet after true prevention; recovery and state-assignment loss excluded |\n| actualDamageDealt | NEW anti-overkill bridge: tempAbsorbed plus actualHpDamage; numeric actual-damage drains/reflections |\n\nOrdinary “damages,” “takes damage” and “damaging hit” predicates use a positive resolved packet. Actual-HP wording (Burning Blood/Rewind Wound) uses actualHpDamage. Numeric actual-damage wording (Sanguine Rite/Echo Leech/Merciful Smite/Wound Eater/Gravebrand) uses actualDamageDealt. Source attack/hit predicates remain distinct.\n\nExample: HP10/temp10 takes5: numeric actual damage5, actualHP damage0, positive packet. HP1 under a floor takes5: numeric0 but positive packet. HP3 takes12: numeric3, raw post-temp12. HP0 takes1: numeric0, positive packet and normal death-failure consequences.\n\nDivine Judgment consumes its once-this-actual-target-turn entitlement on a positive packet, then reflects min(Cleric level, max(1, floor(actualDamageDealt/2))). Thus HP1-floor/numeric0 reflects1 and consumes; fully prevented resolved0 does neither. Wrathful Brand/Balanced Scales use the positive predicate where their source says damage occurred.\n\nResolve a damage event in this order:\n\n1. Snapshot pre-event HP/tempHP/Wounds; resolve defenses, temp absorption and uncapped post-temp damage.\n2. Core Instant Death uses postTempDamage minus preHP greater than or equal to effective maximum when reduced to0. It precedes ordinary floors. Open a legal would-die replacement; stop that packet's death procedure. No Wound save or zero-HP failure follows this branch.\n3. If not Instant Death, resolve applicable true floors. They cannot raise an already0 creature. They preserve raw damage and attached conditions; a prevented drop creates no drop-only Wound trigger, but half-max/Maiming still applies.\n4. Spiral-TouchedIII alone has a specific pre-Wound recovery exception. If positive-to-zero remains, spend its use, preserve that raw crossing trigger, attempt quarter-max direct recovery and apply phase-out. Broad HP-gain prohibition blocks the recovery but not the use/phase.\n5. Resolve the one Core Wound save using original post-temp damage and pre-event Wounds. Fatal Wound death opens its own legal replacement window. Actual death ends Spiral phase; a successful independent Ward prevents actual death.\n6. Resolve already-zero damage failures/death and its legal replacement window. A successful replacement stops skipped same-packet death branches; never replay a Wound/failure branch merely because replacement changedHP.\n7. Finalize a newly surviving0 creature's dying/Stable/knockout state. Only an explicitly triggered post-zero Reaction such as Grace Reversal can act before ordinary zeroHP Reaction loss finalizes.\n8. Resolve eligible post-zero Phoenix/Unyielding-style healing only if target still lives, followed by later triggers. This healing never retroactively reduces the triggering packet's numeric damage.\n\nThe affected creature chooses among simultaneously eligible offered survival effects, then rechecks remaining triggers. A paid Reaction spends its declared cost; a limited automatic survival use spends when its actual source resolves. A true floor preventing the crossing means Spiral's use is not spent. No generic extra Reaction or return from death is created.\n\nFor Ritual Ward, sufficient body and no explicitly unpreventable death are prerequisites. It can intercept Instant Death, fatal Wounds, third death-save failure, eligible direct death and lethal tracks. Positive pre-eventHP→1 is loss prevention and works through Decay. Already0 with HP gain prohibited instead becomes living Stable0: clear counters and establish a d4-hour recovery deadline immediately.\n\nFreeze packetHpLossBeforeReplacement after true floors but before Ward. Ward metrics are min(packetHpLossBeforeReplacement, max(0, preHP minus replacementHP)); never include loss caused solely by assigning a lowerHP state. HP20 takes10 then fatal Wound/Ward1 means actualHP damage10, not19. HP5 takes100 then Ward1 means actualHP damage4, raw100.\n\nDirect death (e.g. Wound EaterIII) produces no damage or Wound save. Its healing requires actual death; a Ward interception prevents that trigger without refunding the paid activation. False LifeI has only its printed floor and failed-death-save replacement modes sharing1/LR; it is not an all-causes Ward.\n\n## ZeroHP permissions and start timing\n\nA conscious but dying creature still rolls at each regular start unless Stable. Extra turns do not advance these regular-start deadlines. Further damage and actual death retain normal consequences.\n\nMage Undying Spellform partially overrides only zeroHP Unconscious/Incapacitated restrictions: normal speech, voluntary half-Speed movement, normal Action, legal spellcasting with that Action, and starting/maintaining concentration. No independent BA/Reaction. Existing concentration avoids automatic loss from zeroHP, but the triggering damage still requires its concentration save. Separate Stunned/Unconscious/Expedition5 or6/Silence and other restrictions still apply. Healing above0 ends the feature. Expiry at0 destabilizes, cancels the Stable recovery deadline and adds one failure; the third kills normally and permits only an eligible would-die replacement.\n\nOracle False LifeIII permits exactly one choice during the allowed zeroHP turn: move up to Speed, speak, or activate one Revelation. The latter uses its printed Action/BA/trigger (trigger must occur on that turn), costs and components. It can start concentration; the feature did not preserve earlier concentration. No independent actions accompany the choice.\n\nFalse LifeV quarter-max direct recovery resolves at its next regular start before the death save. Blocked recovery leaves0 without invented stability; dead creatures do not revive. Spiral phase expires at its next regular start before normal start processing. Fate-BoundIV rerolls a failed death save before committing natural1/counters; use the new result. Its generic death-effect save bonus is not an ordinary death-save modifier. A natural20 whose1HP is blocked uses the previously reviewed Decay bridge: Stable0, cleared counters, immediate d4-hour deadline and preserved used-turn marker. It does not spend Ward. This differs from blocked False LifeV, which remains dying.\n\n## Ordinary healing arithmetic\n\nDetermine legal source, recipients, costs, clocks and available entitlements before arithmetic. The helper receives already eligible modifiers; it does not acquire class powers or validate every source for its caller.\n\nPre-resolution modifiers include Words of Equilibrium's formula M, Zealot's Fervor +S, Throne's Grace +1/die capped S+M, Benevolent Spirits' eligible paid-technique +M, Life Prophecy maximizing dice, and Crane's applicable +S. Conditional add-ons include Ground, Radiance, Aura of Faith, Ember Mantle, Life Linked's rolled1d4, Ranger Healing Touch, Aura of Mercy, Garden's stored+1 and Tranquil Lotus Step.\n\nFirst compute without conditional add-ons. Only if this preview actually restores at least1HP, recompute that same event once with its eligible add-ons. The preview changes no pools/uses. Base5 minus5 plus a conditional3 remains0; eligible pre-resolution Benevolent3 yields3. Multiple flat class bonuses use the strongest across both passes unless an explicit source exception allows stacking. Rolled extra dice/per-die changes are separate.\n\nWithin each permitted reduction plan: formula/bonuses; per-die adjustments; compatible exact fractional multipliers with one floor after multiplication; compatible flat reductions; floor at0; broad HP prohibition; maximum/Critical/reserve caps. Intrinsic Wound Eater and systemic Corrupted remain independent factors. Duplicate same-stat per-die penalties use the strongest. Dice remain dice when maximized/shared; flat Lay on Hands and stored numeric Echoes have zero healing dice.\n\nBlight Totem cannot compound other local healing reducers. Compare systemic factors plus Totem-half alone against systemic factors plus all otherwise compatible local reducers; use the lower result. Compatible halves multiply, compatible distinct flat penalties add, duplicate same-profile groups use strongest. Suppressed reducers do not participate.\n\nEvery present, operative, applicable next-positive-healing reduction consumes exactly once when the final event commits if its pre-reduction HP potential was positive. This includes reducers whose arithmetic branch lost to Totem or whose actual gain was later blocked. Zero potential and suppressed records consume none. Two-pass preview never consumes twice.\n\nCritical at3Wounds caps ordinary healing at max(1, effective maximum minus1); it is not a maximumHP reduction and healing cannot lower currentHP. Reserve spends only actualHP restored. A final heal8 with room3 spends3 reserve; blocked healing spends0, other activation costs remain paid. Revival/form changes/encounter starts do not replenish reserve.\n\n## Recovery gates, triggers and loops\n\nFalse Life curse gates another creature's healing SPELL: optional Insight vs RevelationDC; success permits; failed or declined check rolls d2,1 blocks HP and2 permits. Nonspell recovery and independent nonHP components remain distinct. Burning Blood blocks the cold-tagged HP-healing component only, not independent tempHP/stabilization/resurrection.\n\nActual positiveHP restoration is required for Radiance's cure, Renewal's Stamina, Sanctified Reprisal and Life Prophecy's RC trigger. Meditative Healing's laterKi and Transcendent Meditation's second-pulseKi are independent components and still resolve when healing caps/blocks. Blood Tithe uses its actual qualifying hostile Bleed-death trigger and finite use; not staged farming.\n\nPassive regeneration includes Bloodthick Node, Rapid Coagulation, Regenerative Mutation and Bio-Stabilizer's modification; these retain Reserve. Their own Downed/Incapacitated gates remain. A passive-regeneration prohibition is not a universal healing prohibition.\n\nLay on Hands retains its5×Paladin-level finite pool and ordinary Long Rest refill. Base Action spends at most level on one target; Hero1/LR spends the entire remaining pool across at most3, each allocation at least1. No RP conversion. Each link/application can fire at most once/root. Numeric add-ons remain the same packet, creating no new root/child. Legal finite primary healing may trigger unrelated eligible effects.\n\nStronger source flags override generic provenance: Vitality Link's child blocks every further link; Echo Leech blocks secondary healing/resource triggers; Blood Feast blocks damage/resource triggers; Merciful Smite blocks Smite/heal-copy chains; Crown Undying blocks redirection/heal-copy chains; Echoes/Spiral copies retain their explicit exclusions. Genuine excess is max(0, legal post-reduction healing minus OrdinaryMissingHP). Broad HP prohibition makes legal healing and excess0. Critical's ceiling does not redefine missingHP: max20/HP19/Critical/legalheal10 restores0 but has9 genuine excess; the missing1 is not excess. At HP20, legalheal10 has10 excess.\n\nThis quantity alone grants no conversion. A legal converter must satisfy its own costs/caps/cadences. One excess point can fund only one conversion. Source-internal Overflowing Light resolves before outside Cosmic Equilibrium uses remaining excess. Channel Life attaches a packet-wide prohibition on ALL excess-to-tempHP conversion, including another owner's feature. It permits an otherwise eligible Cosmic damage conversion.\n\n**NEW agreed source exclusion:** Unpaid passive regeneration bounded by Mutation Recovery Reserve cannot supply excess-healing conversions unless a future source explicitly grants an exception. This covers Bloodthick Node, Rapid Coagulation, Regenerative Mutation and Bio-Stabilizer as a Bloodthick modifier. FullHP/Critical may compute mathematical excess but grants no Cosmic conversion and spends no Reserve. Do not extend this exclusion to otherwise eligible paid/finite healing merely because it restores0HP.\n\n## Actual resurrection and retained consequences\n\nRequire an actually dead, willing identity, legal source body/anchor, location, target limit, elapsed window, paid cost and no applicable resurrection/soul blocker. ReturnedHP is a life transition, unaffected by ordinary healing multipliers or generic living HP-gain prohibitions. Explicit corpse-local and identity/soul blockers are different scopes.\n\nPreserve Wounds/Injuries, Corruption, intrinsic Oracle Curse, debt/legal-death/body claims and spent resources. ThreeWounds is Critical, not continuous automatic death. Clear death counters/Stable deadline but preserve used turn markers and persistent histories. Caster death/incapacity does not end it |\n\nSoul Bind removal targets pending creature or existing post-death remains as local anchor. With no remains, removal must expressly free the bound identity/soul; invent no physical anchor. No mind control, ownership or indefinite prison. True Resurrection still obeys actual identity/soul barriers, and an already-living identity is ineligible. Complete objects retain countering, Dissonance/Refrain, costs and source access.\n\n## Temporal dependency and validation boundary\n\nSpiral-TouchedV Time Loop is temporal restoration, not generic resurrection. Its discussed participant contract restores recorded position, life/death/HP/temp/counters, Wounds/linked Injuries, Core/custom conditions, RC, per-encounter uses and snapshot Soul Bind state. Post-snapshot death/body destruction is conceptually undone; already-dead-at-snapshot remains dead. Generic corpse-resurrection denial does not automatically block it.\n\nIt does not restore RP/Ki/Conviction/BG, device resources, Mutation Reserve, daily/rest uses, physical consumables or its own spent use. The helper tests that field distinction only. Full equipment/environment topology, corpse/body-part duplication and clock rebasing remain in the dedicated temporal package; Time Loop is not declared fully operational by these tests.\n\nCurrent selected checks:164 healing/death plus120 unchanged Core damage states;150 control including2 new clarification cases;98 custom including14 cure-presence cases;153 exact source records. These are interface tests, not execution of147 complete class cards, numerical balance, whole-game completion or a substitute for human playtesting.\n"},{"file":"DescentReviewChanges/ReviewBible/docs/mechanics/BODY_EQUIPMENT_PLAYTEST.md","sha256":"d93f9cb0ec8a8398560511f23029d38d9b9a8b901e816373579c8f72b0bec1f3","heading":"entire guide","exactText":"# Bodies equipment and carrying\n\n**REVIEWED — authored tabletop body, equipment, carrying and continuous meld procedures.** New integration mechanics remain explicitly provisional; runtime implementation is pending. These procedures connect the [Druid form rules](FORM_TEMP_HP_PLAYTEST.md) and [transformation spells](TRANSFORMATION_SPELL_PLAYTEST.md). They apply to the same character, HP record and physical belongings throughout a change. They grant no additional forms, spell access, actions or resources. The approved books remain preserved.\n\n## Entering and changing forms\n\nBefore paying for a voluntary change, choose a legal form and size, check its space at your current position, and identify any equipment that must be dropped. If the destination or required drops are illegal, the change does not begin and spends nothing. Otherwise pay the source's normal costs and resolve its actual entry effects once. A successful hostile or forced transformation cannot be prevented merely by excess gear: resolve it under its source rules, then reconcile excess equipment by keeping it external, dropping it or recording pending placement. Other source requirements still apply.\n\nYour Druid form uses your own HP, ability scores, saves, proficiency and conditions. It supplies only its printed anatomy and features. Speech does not imply hands: none of the 26 current Druid profiles grants ordinary hand-based equipment use. Of the 12 spell profiles, only Humanoid grants hands. Each profile retains its exact size and movement permissions.\n\n| Change | Equipment procedure |\n|---|---|\n| Root body to first Druid form | Record and meld the equipment physically worn, held or carried by your body, including carried containers and their contents. |\n| Druid form to another Druid form | Keep the same original melded equipment. End the former form's dependent effects and start the new form under its normal rules. Reconcile newly acquired external items with the new anatomy. |\n| A successful spell replaces a Druid or spell form | Close the former transformation and capture the legitimate resulting gear under the new spell's meld rules in one continuous change. There is no moment when you can use the returning equipment. |\n| Shapechange changes its current spell profile | Keep compatible external items. Drop incompatible external items; they do not join the original melded equipment. |\n| Suppression | Follow the spell's suppression procedure. It is not an ending or a fresh capture of equipment. Original melded gear stays inaccessible. |\n| Final reversion, expiry or forced ending | Return to root anatomy, end the source's dependent effects and reconcile surviving equipment as described below. |\n\nThe continuing meld record across Druid-to-Druid changes is a **new provisional integration rule**. It prevents shifting from opening an equipment-use window between forms. A spell replacement retains the already-reviewed close-and-capture procedure.\n\n## What melds\n\nRecord where each item was before the first change: worn in a particular slot, held in a particular hand, or carried in a particular container or on the body. Ownership alone is insufficient. A sword stored in a wagon stays in the wagon when its owner becomes a Hawk. A legitimately borrowed sword in your actual custody can meld and return normally; the lender remains its owner. Changing ownership metadata does not itself move an inaccessible physical item.\n\nA backpack and each of its contents remain distinct items. Count each item's own weight or Load once; a container does not erase its contents. Melded items remain in your custody but are inaccessible. You cannot withdraw or add contents, use components, consume supplies, fire ammunition or activate equipment through the meld.\n\nMelding never repairs damage, reloads a weapon, restores charges, replenishes supplies or creates a replacement item. Destruction, consumption and legitimate transfers remain real. Keep the current condition and quantity of each actual item.\n\n## Using equipment in a form\n\n| Item or effect | Rule |\n|---|---|\n| Original armor and shield | Melded and unusable. Exclude their worn or held AC benefits from the form's normal AC. |\n| Held weapon, tool, focus or material component | Requires actual access and the appropriate anatomy. A form without hands receives no general permission to use it. |\n| Effect requiring an item to be worn, held or equipped | Suspended while the item is melded. |\n| Effect requiring only possession or attunement | May continue only when its exact rule is independent of the lost anatomy and physical use. An unspecified magic bonus is not automatically operative. |\n| Newly acquired armor, harness, collar or container | Must fit the actual size and anatomy and have a legal way to remain attached or carried. Hands are required for held items, not automatically for every wearable item. |\n| Newly acquired external gear when the form changes | Remains external if compatible. Otherwise use the normal drop procedure. It does not silently join an existing meld record. |\n\nThe possession-versus-use distinction and individual compatibility checks are **new provisional defaults**. A specific item or form can supply an explicit exception. Do not infer one from a creature's name, appearance or ability to speak. Item-specific compatibility across the full equipment catalog remains to be authored.\n\n## Returning equipment\n\nResolve a final reversion in this order:\n\n1. Keep current external items that still exist and remain legally equipped or carried by the root body.\n2. Return each surviving melded item that remains in the character's legitimate custody to its recorded former slot or container, if available and legal.\n3. If an external item now occupies that slot, leave it there. Return the former item as carried inventory if there is a legal place and sufficient capacity.\n4. Drop an item that cannot be carried or equipped at the current position or the nearest legal adjacent item position.\n5. If no legal drop position exists, record that item's placement as pending. It continues to exist; it is not duplicated, destroyed or used to displace another creature.\n\nCurrent external equipment taking priority over a former slot is a **new provisional integration rule**. A returned sword may go into available carried storage instead of replacing a sword you actually acquired and equipped during the transformation. Both retain their current condition and ownership.\n\nDestroyed, consumed, physically transferred, lost or otherwise unavailable items never reappear. An item in another custodian's possession cannot return merely because you still claim ownership of it. Someone else's legal ownership does not prevent an intact borrowed item from returning to your continuing legitimate custody.\n\n## Placement and involuntary endings\n\nA transformation does not grant movement. A voluntary size change keeps the same spatial anchor and must fit before payment. Preserve the existing footprint on a same-size change when possible.\n\nA mandatory ending still occurs if the root anatomy does not fit. First try its normal space at the same position, then a legal squeeze under the [movement rules](COMBAT_ACTION_PLAYTEST.md). If neither fits, record a placement conflict. The **new provisional placement procedure** permits only a nearest legal adjacent placement in the same unobstructed region. It cannot pass through a wall, closed barrier or occupied space. Choose the minimum displacement that fits the whole body; among ties, preserve the greatest overlap with its previous footprint. If still tied, the Director uses a fixed neutral board order, not a tactical choice by the affected player. If no adjacent candidate fits, the conflict remains until the obstruction is removed or a legal relocation effect resolves it. Do not search farther afield.\n\nThis mandatory normalization spends no movement and triggers no opportunity attack, charge distance, movement benefit or other movement rider. It changes no remaining movement allowance and grants no invented crushing damage. It does not permit a voluntary form change to bypass its preflight.\n\nTrack actual height and terrain. Losing flight while unsupported starts ordinary falling from the current location; reversion never moves the creature directly to the ground. Ending a burrowing form underground does not move the creature to the surface. Master of Vermin's permission to pass through Tiny gaps changes clearance, not its printed size.\n\nUse the actual space of the expressed anatomy:\n\n| Size | Space | On a grid of 2.5-foot subcells |\n|---|---|---|\n| Tiny | 2.5 by 2.5 feet | 1 subcell |\n| Small | 5 by 5 feet | 2 by 2 subcells |\n| Medium | 5 by 5 feet | 2 by 2 subcells |\n| Large | 10 by 10 feet | 4 by 4 subcells |\n\nV6 explicitly supplies Tiny and Medium space. Small and Large complete this bounded table provisionally; the supplied V6 passage does not separately list them. Size category still matters even when two sizes occupy the same space. Squeezing changes clearance and its penalties; it does not change size, carrying capacity or weight. Exact Tiny occupancy requires subcells or equivalent measurement, not treating Tiny as Medium.\n\n## Body weight and conditions\n\n**New provisional rule:** a creature-form transformation preserves the body's existing base physical weight unless an exact rule changes it. Size alone does not change weight. A 180-pound character therefore remains 180 pounds as a Tiny Hawk or Large Bear. This is a magical transformation rule, not a claim about the weight of ordinary animals. It applies equally to the current 26 Druid and 12 creature-form spell profiles.\n\nRecord base body weight separately from equipment and condition effects. Petrified multiplies current body weight by ten once; it does not rewrite the base weight or multiply repeatedly for multiple applications. Ending or changing a form does not cure that condition. Temporary Druid form ends under its existing Incapacitated rule, then Petrified continues to apply to the reverted body. An anatomy permitted to persist keeps its own base weight and the same condition overlay.\n\nTrue Polymorph object anatomy uses its actual documented output weight. Its output ceiling remains the lower of current effective input weight and earliest recorded origin weight. Include a current applicable weight-changing condition in the input, but never rebase the origin ceiling upward. Equipment, casualty Load and estimated pounds do not establish material available for a transformation. Creature-origin object anatomy has no independent sale, salvage or harvest value.\n\nFor another creature, mount, floor or platform supporting you, count your effective body weight plus all actual equipment in your custody, including melded items, once. For your own inventory capacity, count what you carry, not your own body weight. Conditions change only the items or body their exact rules affect.\n\n## Carrying and Load\n\nUse pounds for actual weight and Load for inventory burden. Extraction Class describes cargo danger and attention. A casualty's Load describes the difficulty of transporting a body; it is not the body's weight. These quantities answer different questions and never convert automatically into each other.\n\nThe following is a **new provisional reconciliation** of V6 physical handling with the staged Gameplay Loop inventory rules. It does not promote the historical Gameplay Loop file into the approved books. V6's optional encumbrance variant remains preserved as an alternative; do not add its penalties to this Load procedure.\n\n| Carrier size | Physical carry limit in pounds | Push, drag or lift limit in pounds | Inventory capacity in Load | Casualty Load for a body of this size |\n|---|---|---|---|---|\n| Tiny | Strength score × 7.5 | Strength score × 15 | Strength score ÷ 2 | 1.5 |\n| Small | Strength score × 15 | Strength score × 30 | Maximum of 0 and Strength score − 2 | 3 |\n| Medium | Strength score × 15 | Strength score × 30 | Strength score | 6 |\n| Large | Strength score × 30 | Strength score × 60 | Strength score × 2 | 12 |\n\nUse the character's current Strength score, not the modifier or an imported monster score. Tiny's Inventory capacity and casualty Load are new provisional additions. The pound limits come from V6; Small, Medium and Large Load values come from the Gameplay Loop. Larger unsupported transport sizes require their own completed rule before this table can handle them.\n\nKeep fractional Loads without rounding. An item with a printed Load uses that value. If it has only a known weight, its **provisional Load is pounds divided by ten**. A Load-only item may use ten pounds per Load as a clearly labeled estimate for ordinary hauling; this is insufficient for effects requiring documented physical mass.\n\nCount a container's own empty Load plus the contents' Loads, unless an explicitly inclusive Load already covers those exact contents. Handle pounds separately: sum the empty container weight and contents' weights, or use an explicitly inclusive gross weight for the contents it covers. An inclusive Load does not make a printed weight inclusive, or vice versa. Apply this rule through nested containers and meld budgets so every item counts exactly once in each quantity.\n\nWith positive carried Load at or above half capacity and no greater than capacity, you are **Burdened**: reduce Speed by 10 feet and apply disadvantage to checks to climb, swim or move silently. An empty inventory is not Burdened, including at zero capacity. Above capacity, that inventory cannot be carried routinely; drop or transfer something, or use a legal alternative such as dragging or a vehicle. These threshold and zero-Load details are provisional clarifications.\n\nApply actual physical limits when handling a known weight. Between physical carry capacity and twice that capacity, pushing or dragging uses the lower of otherwise-current Speed and 5 feet. A Speed of 0 stays 0; a Speed of 3 stays 3. Lifting does not independently permit movement: moving while supporting a weight must satisfy carrying, pushing, dragging or another exact rule. An anatomy restriction, such as Hawk's prohibition on carrying a Medium ally, still applies even if numbers would fit. Burden penalties do not create hands, harnesses, lift or access to melded gear.\n\nTransporting a creature must pass both checks: the carrier can physically handle the body's effective weight and associated equipment, and can accommodate the casualty Load plus the equipment's Load. Petrified multiplies the body weight by ten, not its casualty Load. Removing gear can reduce equipment weight and Load; it cannot reduce the body's weight or erase its condition.\n\n## Meld limits across repeated transformations\n\n**New provisional meld exception:** legally melded gear is excluded from both your transformed body's active Inventory Load and its own active pound-carrying limit. Record its full Load and weight separately while it remains inaccessible. New external equipment uses the expressed form's current capacities and still needs compatible anatomy and custody.\n\nA **continuous meld chain** begins when root anatomy first enters a transformation that melds gear. At that instant, record two fixed ceilings: the pre-transformation body's ordinary active Load capacity and its ordinary physical carrying capacity in pounds. Legally melded gear must fit both. Keep the actual total Load and pounds of the hidden gear, counting containers and contents once in each quantity.\n\nDruid-to-Druid changes, internal Shapechange switches, suppression, permanent True Polymorph and continuous replacement by another spell do not end the chain or increase its ceilings. Later changes to size or Strength neither increase nor retroactively shrink these historical ceilings. An existing item is not expelled merely to make room for a new one.\n\nA later spell replacement may capture an external item only if the whole item fits the remaining room under **both** ceilings. Do not partially meld an item unless its source defines a divisible cargo quantity. A new item that will not fit remains external if compatible with the destination and within its current carrying limits; otherwise drop it or, for a forced resolution without legal placement, record its placement as pending. Excess cargo never grants immunity to a hostile or forced transformation. Druid-to-Druid changes continue to preserve their existing meld and never recapture external cargo.\n\nFor a voluntary change, declare and preflight the full item disposition before spending costs. A successful hostile or forced change resolves its mandatory custody consequences without deleting items or refunding its costs. This same overflow procedure applies at the first forced transformation if the target began with excess cargo; only gear within both starting ceilings can become hidden.\n\nWhen someone else carries the transformed character, count its casualty Load plus both its melded and external equipment Load. Count the effective physical body weight plus all that equipment for actual weight handling. Melding therefore provides no weightless cargo for another carrier.\n\nOnly an actual final return to root anatomy, with the hidden equipment released and reconciled, ends the chain. Returned gear rejoins active inventory under the root body's **current** capacities. Resolve excess with the return-and-drop procedure; overload cannot prevent a mandatory reversion. A later new transformation can then establish a new pair of ceilings from its actual legal starting state.\n\n## Extraction Class and Secure Cargo\n\nExtraction Class is a danger and logistics rating, not a carrying allowance:\n\n| Class | Cargo |\n|---|---|\n| 0 | Ordinary trade goods |\n| 1 | Common monster parts or durable salvage |\n| 2 | Uncommon, fragile or regulated cargo |\n| 3 | Rare artifacts, volatile substances or living captives |\n| 4 | Very rare strategic cargo or dangerous curses |\n| 5 | Epic cargo likely to attract sovereign or faction intervention |\n| 6 | Legendary cargo requiring a dedicated extraction operation |\n\nRetain relevant Fragile, Volatile, Cursed, Living, Contraband, Perishable and Attention tags. A light object can carry a high Extraction Class, and a heavy ordinary crate can remain Class 0.\n\n**New provisional wording for Secure Cargo:** spend an Action on one legal attach, lift, transfer or protective operation involving one discrete cargo unit or bundle with an assigned Extraction Class. The unit must be physically handleable as declared; naming an entire convoy a bundle does not make it one operation. The action grants no extra capacity, hands, equipment, automatic success, invulnerability or reduction in Extraction Class. Apply actual restraints, harnesses, checks, hazards and source-specific handling requirements.\n\n## Examples\n\n**The remote sword.** A Druid wears armor, holds a staff and carries a pack containing two potions. A spare sword is in a wagon. The armor, staff, pack and both potions meld when the Druid changes. The sword remains in the wagon. Neither potion can be consumed from the melded pack.\n\n**A compatible harness.** A transformed creature acquires a harness made for its present anatomy. A later change checks the harness against the destination form. If it does not fit, the voluntary change requires a legal drop. The harness is neither erased nor added to the old melded equipment.\n\n**The crowded hand.** A spell Humanoid acquires and holds a new weapon. When the spell ends, root anatomy can keep that weapon if legal. The original melded weapon returns to an available legal carrying position, or is dropped if necessary. It does not replace or duplicate the new weapon.\n\n**The loaded Hawk.** A Strength 9 Druid becomes Tiny with 3 Load of legally melded gear. It stays inaccessible and uses neither of the Hawk's active carrying capacities. Tiny capacity is 4.5 Load and 67.5 pounds. An additional package of 3 Load and 30 pounds in a genuinely compatible external carrying arrangement fits both capacities, but exceeds half the Load capacity of 2.25 and makes the Druid Burdened. A rescuer carrying that Hawk counts 1.5 casualty Load plus all 6 Load of equipment, totaling 7.5 Load, and separately satisfies the full body and equipment weight.\n\n**The repeated replacement.** A Strength 9 Medium character starts a chain with 8 Load and 80 pounds of legal gear. The fixed ceilings are 9 Load and 135 pounds. The remaining hidden capacity is therefore 1 Load and 55 pounds. A later spell cannot hide a new 3-Load, 30-pound package even if the current form can carry it externally. A whole 0.5-Load, 50-pound item fits both remaining ceilings; a 0.5-Load, 60-pound item fails the pound limit. Changing form or casting again does not refresh either ceiling.\n\n**The stone casualty.** A Medium ally has a documented base weight of 180 pounds and becomes Petrified. The body now weighs 1,800 pounds but still contributes 6 casualty Load. A Strength 18 Medium rescuer can normally carry 270 pounds or push, drag or lift 540 pounds, so cannot move that body alone through these ordinary procedures, even with enough spare Load capacity. Additional gear is counted separately.\n\n## Source decisions and implementation status\n\nThe [first body and equipment conference](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_TEMPORAL_BODY_EQUIPMENT_CONFERENCE.md) supplies the equipment procedures. The [additional exact evidence](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/CODEX_TEMPORAL_CARRYING_EVIDENCE.md) and [GPT's completed reconciliation](../../../../docs/audit/2026-10-08/full-game/GPT_TEMPORAL_CARRYING_RECONCILIATION.md) preserve the V6, Gameplay Loop and Core conflicts and their dispositions. The corrected candidate above replaces the first conference's full-cell Tiny footprint, factor-four mass proposal and automatic active Load from melded equipment. The manuscript review's chain budgets, pound exemption, borrowed-custody correction, separate inclusive quantities and neutral placement tie rule are applied here. The spell helper's coarse hands check does not implement the full compatibility procedure. The finite ground-plane temporal helper does not model elevation, burrowing or Tiny subcells. It closes no full class, form, temporary-HP system or complete runtime by itself. [Source register](CREATURE_LIFECYCLE_SOURCE_BINDINGS.md), [source integrity](CREATURE_LIFECYCLE_SOURCE_VALIDATION.json), [behavior checks](CREATURE_LIFECYCLE_PLAYTEST_VALIDATION.json).\n\nThe original class cards and frozen class guide remain intact. The notebook's first classification, boundary reconciliation and event-model decisions are preserved in the audit folder. Where the historical first reply differs from the later boundary replies, the later explicit resolution controls. In particular, Auto-Raise is automatic, its new minion cannot act at all in the creation round, and Mirrorsage Charm was not in the older 219-source control closure.\n\n## At the table\n\nKeep one companion command and one offensive companion allowance per owner per numbered round, including multiclass companions and devices. A normal command costs your Bonus Action. A damaging companion action, or Mage/Wizard control action, spends the offensive allowance. A pure support command spends only the command. The earlier device ledger's internal field named `damage` now represents this shared offensive allowance; its public behavior remains compatible with existing device checks.\n\nIssue ordinary commands during your regular turn. The selected companion moves and performs its command immediately after your whole turn, in an attached phase. It receives no extra independent turn. If it or its target becomes ineligible before execution, the command fails without refund. Your later incapacity or removal alone does not cancel an issued command; actual concentration/source ending can. Uncommanded companions can move defensively and Dodge, subject to their conditions; this grants no generic Reaction or autonomous attack/control.\n\nUse the owner's original regular companion markers for recharge and previous/next-turn references. Extra turns, initiative changes and removal do not restart those markers. The attached phase's event-resolution checkpoint is distinct: it refreshes no Reaction, recharge, Action, death save or regular duration clock.\n\n## Different kinds of creature life\n\n| Creature | HP and 0 HP | Return and identity |\n|---|---|---|\n| Finite standard summons, Druid swarms, Mage Familiar, Mirror Beasts | HP-only bounded layer; at 0 disappear/end | No corpse, resource or free resummon; a new summon pays its actual source costs |\n| Ranger Beast | HP-only; **NEW** immediate death at 0, no Wound checks, Stable or death saves | Actual animal and ordinary surviving corpse; death ends bond. No automatic resurrection or rebond |\n| Mage Lich elite | Destroyed bound asset at 0; no usable corpse/resources | Same unique asset remains owned. Soulbound or actual completed LR can return it |\n| Wizard standard undead | Destroyed at 0; consumed body never returns as new fuel | Ends on destruction, dismissal or owner's next completed LR, including Auto-Raised minions |\n| Wizard elite | Supplied profile/cap only in this package | Binding/acquisition and return procedure are still missing; no create/bind/return API |\n| Reflective Companion | Original creature's HP and life rules | Temporary control relation; no copied body, reset, new statistics or duplicate turn |\n| Druid form | Same character's HP/life rules | Full forms and Buffer Reserve remain a separate open interface |\n\nCompanion healing restores legal normal HP only. It does not reset identity, duration, recharge, actions, source uses, effects or command budgets. Ranger's “heal normally” supplies no Hit Dice pool or automatic rest healing. The HP-only layer is specific to this reviewed companion chassis, not generic law for all animals/NPCs. Existing animal conditions, disease, poison, explicit injuries and timed effects survive bonding.\n\n## Stats and ownership\n\n**NEW:** finite created creatures snapshot base HP and AC at creation. Attacks, saves, class DC and S use the current values of the original source owner at resolution. Keep source owner, current controller, source class and creation source separate. A temporary controller does not donate their class levels, PB or attack bonus. The implementation rejects stats belonging to another owner/class; it provides no unrestricted transfer/rebinding operation.\n\nStandard profile: AC 12 + floor(PB/2), HP 2L, Speed 30, owner PB for saves, one class attack for S d6 with printed melee/ranged mode. Druid swarm uses current PB+WIS, piercing and its printed tiny-gap movement. Familiar snapshots AC 12+PB and HP 5+Mage level+M; its current source-owner Mage attack fires a 30-foot Elemental Dart for S d4. Focus is fixed at creation: Lightning adds Fly30, Fire/Cold their matching resistance. Changing Focus neither morphs nor refills it. One Familiar; replacement ends the prior one.\n\nRanger maximum is **NEW dynamic** max(1, 2×current Ranger level + actual WIS modifier), not M. Current PB updates its standard AC/saves/attack. Persistent Mage elite maxima use current source-owner Mage level; fixed AC stays fixed. Capacity growth never heals; shrink clamps HP without damage. Wizard elite profiles use the same names with their own original Wizard source statistics if a future legitimate binding source establishes them.\n\nThe [reviewed maximum-HP ledger](MAXIMUM_HP_PLAYTEST.md) supplies source-qualified modifiers. Conjuration is the original creating Wizard level snapshot and requires actual Wizard spell creation. Corpse Harvest qualifies only with actual access; Auto-Raise is not a spell and grants no new Conjuration snapshot. Flesh Ascendant is the explicit dynamic eligible-current-controller exception; engine and card are one effect, not two bonuses. Creation starts at full final legal maximum unless the actual source enters damaged; later added capacity never fills HP. Callers update dynamic modifier records from their real sources.\n\n## Ranger's persistent bond\n\nUse the printed one-hour ritual, touch, 4 RP and one long-rest use. The animal must already be willing/tamed, noncorrupted, INT below7 and CR no greater than Ranger level. The helper does not invent finding/taming rules. Keep one chosen movement mode, capped40 feet, and the original keen-sense identity; no monster stat block, boss powers or free special actions. Exact keen-sense selection definitions remain an evidence gap.\n\n**NEW initial conversion:** for a living animal, new HP = max(1, floor(new maximum × old current HP / old maximum)). A wounded animal stays proportionally wounded. This is profile conversion, not healing or damage. A living bound Beast cannot be rebonded to refill HP. On death, ordinary legal resurrection may restore the same animal under its own source, but it remains unbonded. A new full ritual also requires an actual completed long rest after bond death.\n\nPhysical body identity is separate from current corpse eligibility. Keep one record whose state is living, corpse, consumed or removed. Accepting an already-resolved, independently legal resurrection immediately marks that same body living and unavailable as a corpse, before any rebond. Corpse Gateway cannot use it while living. A later genuine death with a surviving body makes that same record a corpse again; no fragment/new body identity is generated. A consumed/removed body requires explicit reconstruction by the actual resurrection source before it can return. The notification helper grants no resurrection ability, payment exemption or automatic bond. Rebond uses the actual post-resurrection HP ratio and preserves death history; it cannot claim a healthier ratio. Full global corpse decay/cleanup remains open.\n\n## Mage Lich assets and elite commands\n\nAn actual successful Lich transformation unlocks Boneflayer, Dreadblade and Wraithbound. Readiness and informed retirement-risk consent are source guards; this lifecycle helper does not perform that irreversible ritual. **NEW shared identity:** across Mage/Wizard, one owner can have at most one of each named elite and three total. Destroyed assets still occupy those identities. Any future external Wizard binding must validate against the same asset register; the current helper offers only its profile schema.\n\n| Elite | Profile | Source-native actions/riders |\n|---|---|---|\n| Boneflayer | AC14, HP12+2L, Speed30; ranged60, 2d6+S necrotic | Fear Pulse replaces attack, enemies10 feet WIS or Frightened through target-next-end, recharge3. Grave Miasma replaces attack: radius10 point30 until next owner-start, enemies entry/start1d6 once, recharge3 |\n| Dreadblade | AC16, HP16+3L, Speed30; melee5, 1d8+S necrotic | Concentrating hit target immediately saves with disadvantage; natural20 applies printed −1 attack Weakened until target-next-end. Advantage against forced movement |\n| Wraithbound | AC14, HP10+2L, Fly40/hover; melee5, 1d8+S necrotic | Natural20 CON or Stunned until target-next-end under shared character→target hard-denial guard |\n\nRolls, chosen enemy geometry, recharge payments, exact condition applications and canonical hard-guard sharing remain caller/source responsibilities. This helper returns attack profiles and bounded lifecycle/command transitions; it does not silently execute every on-hit effect.\n\n**NEW Miasma integration:** creation spends one offensive command. Its native finite entry/start damage needs no second command and does not spend next round's offense. Retain no-resource/no-recursion flags. An old zone tick is not a new companion turn or attack.\n\nWraithbound loses source invisibility on attack **or control**. It can regain it at the end of its next original regular companion marker if the predicate remains satisfied. A current-marker end cannot immediately restore it. The actual offense event must notify its marker state. Core Invisible still does not grant unknown location, sight through walls or automatic target legality.\n\nSoulbound requires the destroyed own bound elite within30 or its inert token, legal empty space30, Action, 5 RP and one LR use. Return the same asset/instance at **NEW source-specific** max(1, floor(current maximum/2)), plus S+M temp HP for60 seconds under normal temp-HP rules. Do not copy machine reconstruction permissions. Preserve spent uses, recharge clocks, command history and still-applicable effects. Completed LR returns those same Mage assets at full current HP unless an actual campaign consequence prevents it. Rest-use/recharge resets come only from the canonical rest system; a return itself grants none. Natural effect expiries must still be processed.\n\n## Wizard corpses, standard minions and Auto-Raise\n\nCorpse Harvest costs Action+4 RP, recharge2, legal corpse/space30. Body reservation does not prevent another legal consumer from taking the body first. A destroyed/expired killer does not cancel an already-created reservation.\n\nResolve after immediate death responses and already-due end damage/saves, before ordinary expiry cleanup, in stable application order.
